and &quot;grl-7&quot; (hedgehog-related protein) in the options strongly points to *C. elegans* biology<br>- In *C. elegans*, PTC-1 is the Patched homolog that, unlike mammalian PTCH1, does NOT signal to activate hedgehog pathway<br>- Instead, PTC-1 functions as a transporter that promotes endocytosis-mediated degradation of hedgehog-related proteins (grl-7, grl-8, grl-11, grl-12, qfp-1)<br>- This degradation activity is critical for maintaining quiescence of progenitor cells during L1 arrest (starvation-induced developmental arrest)<br><br>**Evaluating Options:**<br>- **A**: Describes NPC1L1 (cholesterol transporter) - doesn&#x27;t match the Patched-family architecture or the C. elegans context<br>- **B**: Correctly describes PTC-1&#x27;s known function in *C. elegans* - transporter promoting degradation of hedgehog-related proteins to maintain quiescence<br>- **C**: Describes a hedgehog receptor activating proliferation - this contradicts PTC-1&#x27;s actual inhibitory/degradative role<br>- **D**: Describes a lysosomal enzyme - inconsistent with the multi-TM transporter architecture observed<br><br>2. answer: B</td>
